- **prototype**: Use before `scope`, when nobody knows yet whether an idea is worth building — a new library, an unfamiliar API, an interaction nobody has seen on screen. Builds the shortest thing that answers two questions, does it work and does it show something, in a throwaway git worktree, and leaves a report in `docs/` the project owner decides on.
